I found nothing blocking. The privacy rules, grants and security definer functions are sound. I checked reads two ways: a sibling (C2 reading C's rows) and another family's parent (PB) both get nothing, because `is_parent_of` requires an approved parent and an approved child in the same family. Every function sets `search_path = ''`. The five child-facing functions are `authenticated` only, and the internal helper `learning_child_family` is revoked from every role. Every foreign key and every query in `repository.ts` is covered by an index. Lock order has no deadlock cycle. Box moves are 1→3→7→14→14, and `family_today` is used for every date. The migration isn't on `main` yet, so these fixes can go into the same file.

The read-only scan for hidden code was clean.

**Should fix**

1. `supabase/migrations/20261030100000_learning_progress.sql:215-224`: `start_mission` reads the open attempt without locking it.
   - If `complete_mission` commits first, the later `update ... 'abandoned'` matches the row again. It then fails `mission_attempts_check` with a 23514 error, which the child sees as an error.
   - A stale client sending an older version also abandons a newer attempt, so two deploy skews can bounce back and forth.
   - Fix: add `for update` to the select, and before the update add `if p_content_version < v_open.content_version then raise exception using errcode = 'JA409', message = 'This mission was updated'; end if;`.

2. `:37-38`: nothing in the schema ties `step_results.child_id` (the column RLS reads) to the attempt's child. Only the function enforces it. Fix:
   ```sql
   -- mission_attempts
   unique (id, child_id)
   -- step_results: replace the attempt_id FK
   foreign key (attempt_id, child_id) references public.mission_attempts (id, child_id) on delete cascade
   ```

3. Child removal (ADR 0007). The cascade is right for the delete-identity path. But when `remove_child` returns false (the child has money history), all six learning tables stay forever and nobody can read them, since `is_parent_of` needs an approved child. That goes against data minimisation for under-14s. Fix: in this migration, `create or replace public.remove_child` and add these after the credential deletes (`20261029130007_ledger_keep_child_history.sql:70-71`). They are idempotent.
   ```sql
   delete from public.mission_attempts where child_id = p_child_id; -- step_results cascade
   delete from public.skill_progress where child_id = p_child_id;
   delete from public.review_items where child_id = p_child_id;
   delete from public.practice_results where child_id = p_child_id;
   delete from public.learning_checkins where child_id = p_child_id;
   ```
   Then update ADR 0007 and docs/database.md.

4. These lists will make later changes hard:
   - The step types are listed twice (`:41` and `:271`).
   - The `correct_count` limit of 5 is in two places (`:105` and `:453`).
   - The key regexes repeat about ten times.
   - Fix: let the table checks be the only validation, and give each a name (`constraint step_results_step_type_chk check (...)`). Make `correct_count` content-agnostic: `check (correct_count >= 0 and correct_count <= jsonb_array_length(answers))`.

5. `:439-463`: the spec (§4.2) says the check-in "sets the starting skill levels", but `complete_checkin` never writes `skill_progress`, and neither does the service. Either seed it (for example, level 1 for each skill in `answers`) or change the spec.

**Nit**

- `:261-279`: `record_step_result` locks the attempt and returns false for a finished one before validating input. Validate first.
- `:125-127`: `authenticated` keeps REFERENCES, TRIGGER and MAINTAIN (Postgres 17). It matches the ledger pattern, but `revoke all ... from authenticated; grant select ... to authenticated;` is tighter.
- `:81`: `review_items_child_due_idx` duplicates the primary key for per-child reads.
- `:421-424`: a right answer on the second try with no hints is stored as `last_result = 'correct_with_hints'`. Rename it to something like `correct_with_help`.
- `:17`: only `mission_attempts` has `family_id`, and nothing reads it.

**Gaps in `supabase/tests/database/learning_progress.test.sql`**

- Box ladder: 3→7, 7→14, 14→14; right with hints keeps the box with due = today + box; tries=2 with no hints doesn't move up.
- `complete_mission` on a skill already in box 3 keeps its schedule (the `on conflict do nothing` path).
- `record_step_result` on an abandoned attempt returns false. Starting a new version after a completion leaves the completed row alone.
- Error 403 (JA403) for a parent, a child in no family, and the other family's child (CB) on `record_step_result`, `complete_mission`, `record_practice_result` and `complete_checkin`. Today only `start_mission` is tested for the parent and the child in no family, and only `record_step_result` for a sibling. Also `complete_mission` by a sibling.
- Lines 201-207 leave `skill_progress` out of the "another family sees nothing" sum. Lines 196-198 check only 2 of the 6 tables for the sibling.
- Removal:
  - After `remove_child`, the parent's reads return 0 and the child's writes get JA403.
  - Deleting the child's auth user removes rows from all six tables, so learning rows never block the ADR 0007 delete.
- Privileges: assert them with `table_privs_are('public', '<t>', 'anon', '{}')`, `table_privs_are(..., 'authenticated', array['SELECT'])` (after the revoke fix), and `function_privs_are` for anon and service_role.
- A skill's level never drops: a wrong answer after reaching level 2 stays 2.

Files reviewed:
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/migrations/20261030100000_learning_progress.sql
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/tests/database/learning_progress.test.sql
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/migrations/20261029130007_ledger_keep_child_history.sql

## Security review: PR #29, AI money guide (`feat/money-guide` against `feat/learning-journey`)

I scanned the diff for obfuscated code first and found none. The lines over 500 characters are all Markdown prose. The diff contains no credentials, and every email address in it is a placeholder.

### High

**H1. A parent can read the child's message that got a safety response.**
- **Where:** `supabase/migrations/20261030120000_guide.sql:94-96`, `src/features/guide/server/service.ts:252-258`.
- **Problem:** The rule that a parent only sees "a safety response was shown" exists only in the app's `hideSafety`. The database read policy (`is_parent_of(child_id)`) still lets any parent select `guide_messages.text`. A parent can call the database API directly with their own session and read a disclosure such as "someone hurt me", even when that parent is the person it is about.
- **Proof:** `supabase/tests/database/guide.test.sql:79-80,108` records exactly that message and asserts the parent can read all 4 rows. The public `/safety` page (`src/app/(marketing)/safety/page.tsx:52`) promises the opposite.
- **Fix:** In `guide_record_turn`, when `p_route = 'safety'`, either store no text for the child's message or mark it with a `parent_hidden` column. Add `and not parent_hidden` to the parent's half of the read policy. Change the test so it asserts the parent cannot read it.

**H2. The "quick check" question is model output that is never screened.**
- **Where:** `pipeline.ts:116-120,137`, `claude.ts:109-128`.
- **Problem:** The output screen only sees `reply.reply`. The model's `check.question`, `options` and `explanation` go straight to the child (`guide-sheet.tsx:344-367`). So a jailbreak that passes the input screen can put a product, a purchase instruction or unsafe text into the options.
- **Made worse by:** `cleanCheck` limits only the question's length. The check is not stored, so parents can't see it. The evals only grade `result.text`, so they don't test it either.
- **Fix:** Send the whole check to the output screen. Cap the length of each field. Store the check with the reply.

### Medium

**M1. When the guide is paused or the input screen fails, a safety message gets no safety handling.**
- **Where:** `service.ts:113-130`, `pipeline.ts:95-98`.
- **Problem:** If the circuit breaker is open, the daily budget is used up or the input screen throws, the message is never classified. A child in crisis gets "The guide is taking a break" with no helplines and no flag, and their parent sees the message in full.
- **Fix:** Always include helplines and "talk to an adult you trust" in the `unavailable` text (`copy.ts:27`). On these paths, run a cheap keyword safety check and treat a match as a safety route.

**M2. Blocked messages are still sent to the main model in later turns, which opens a multi-turn injection path.**
- **Where:** `service.ts:90-93`, `claude.ts:93`.
- **Problem:** Earlier child messages that were routed as jailbreak, unsafe, personal_info or safety go back to Opus as user turns. The input screen judges only the latest message. So a child can plant instructions in one turn, then send a harmless "ok, do that" that passes, and only the output screen stands in the way. Personal details also get re-sent to Anthropic on every turn.
- **Fix:** Pass only the turns whose reply was `on_topic`. Add multi-turn cases to the evals.

### Low

- **No length cap on the model's reply** (`provider.ts:10`). A reply over 2,000 characters breaks the column limit (`migration:50`), so `recordTurn` throws (`service.ts:137`). The child sees an error, and the tokens are never stored, so the daily budget undercounts. Fix: cap the reply in the schema and pipeline, and record usage on failure.
- **Child text can close the XML tags in the screen prompts** (`claude.ts:46-57,114-127`). The text sits raw inside `<latest_child_message>` and `<child_message>`. Fix: escape `<` and `>`.
- **Scrubber gaps** (`scrub.ts:16`):
  - It misses social handles ("@name", "snap: x") and many domain endings (.gg, .tv, .xyz).
  - Messages routed `personal_info` are still stored for 30 days. Fix: store a placeholder for that route.
- **Fake-provider guard** (`config.ts:15`): it only checks `VERCEL_ENV`. On any non-Vercel production host, `GUIDE_PROVIDER=fake` would give real children keyword-only safety routing. Fix: also require an explicit test-only flag.
- **Eval workflow** (`guide-evals.yml:23-25,29`):
  - Actions are pinned to version tags, not commit SHAs.
  - `pnpm install` runs dependency install scripts in the same workspace before the step that holds the key.
  - Anyone with write access can start a run on a modified branch and get the key.
  - Fix: pin SHAs, install with `--ignore-scripts`, and put the secret in a GitHub environment that needs a reviewer's approval.
- **The browser decides the try count** (`schemas.ts:22`). A child can send `wrongTries: 2` and get the answer straight away. This only affects learning, not safety.

### Checked, nothing found

- **Prompt-injection paths for the context:** it is built only from content keys, enums, integers and authored text. A child's answer is mapped to authored option text or formatted numbers.
- **Streaming:** none. The child sees nothing until the output screen passes.
- **Authorization:**
  - Asking is child-only: `assertRole("child")`, then a check that the conversation's `childId` matches the user, plus RLS.
  - The switch is parent-only: `assertParentOf` in the app and `is_parent_of` inside `set_guide_access`.
  - Transcripts are reached only through `listGuideChats(childId)`.
  - Siblings and other families are kept apart by `is_self` and `is_parent_of`.
  - The admin client is used only after these checks, with ids taken from the session.
- **Grants:** the three server-only functions are revoked from `public`, `anon` and `authenticated`. `blank_old_guide_text` is revoked from everyone, including `service_role`. Tables are read-only for signed-in users.
- **API key:** read only on the server through `env.server.ts` (which imports `server-only`). It is never `NEXT_PUBLIC_`. Errors are logged by class name only.
- **Logging:** only route, model, tokens and latency.
- **Rate limits:** an atomic per-child counter keyed on the session user id, checked before any model call. The 12-turn cap is enforced in the database with a row lock.
- **Eval report:** made-up cases and model replies only, and it is gitignored.

**PR #29 review: feat/money-guide vs feat/learning-journey**

Pre-scan: no obfuscation found. Every line over 500 characters is prose in docs, the ADR or the eval judge prompt. No eval/Function/child_process/RPC patterns. The `.gitignore` change only ignores `evals/guide/results/`.

What is clean:
- **Barrels:** the client barrel exposes only the `"use server"` actions, so `claude.ts`, `env.server` and the admin client stay behind the action boundary. `server/index.ts` starts with `import "server-only"`.
- **Layering:** only `repository.ts` names tables and functions.
- **Actions and auth:** every action uses `createAction` with Zod, and every service entry asserts the user (`assertRole`, `assertParentOf` or `assertParentCanRead`).
- **Rendering:** session reads sit inside `<Suspense>`. There is no `console` or `process.env` in `src`.
- **SDK v1.3.0:** `messages.parse`, `zodOutputFormat` (uses `zod/v4`; zod is 4.6.7), `role: "system"` (`SystemMessageParam` is in the `MessageParam` union), `output_config.effort/format`, nullable cache usage fields and `stop_reason: "refusal"` all exist in the installed types.

### Blocking

1. **Parents can read a child's safety-hidden message directly from the database.**
   - Where: `supabase/migrations/20261030120000_guide.sql:94-96`. The parent policy is `is_self(child_id) or is_parent_of(child_id)` on every `guide_messages` row. Hiding is done only in the app (`service.ts:252-258`). The pgTAP test even asserts it (`guide.test.sql:108`: the parent reads all 4 rows, including "someone hurt me").
   - Impact: a parent with their session and the public key can query `guide_messages.text` and read the message. This breaks the ADR 0010 rationale ("without the message going to the person it may be about") and the public claim at `safety/page.tsx:52`.
   - Fix: when `p_route = 'safety'`, have `guide_record_turn` set `private = true` on the child row. Change the policy to `is_self(child_id) or (is_parent_of(child_id) and not private)`. Have `hideSafety` build the placeholder for the missing row, and add a pgTAP assertion that the parent can't read it.

### Should fix

2. **Refusals and truncated output throw instead of being handled (`claude.ts:67-79, 103-105`).**
   - Cause: `messages.parse` parses every text block and throws `AnthropicError` on non-schema JSON (`sdk/lib/parser.js` `parseOutputFormat`). A refusal that has text, or a `max_tokens` cut-off, throws before the `stop_reason === "refusal"` check runs.
   - Result: a refused input screen becomes `unavailable` (not flagged, no helplines), not the `unsafe` the comment promises. It also counts as a breaker failure, so one child sending 5 refused messages a minute (the per-minute cap) pauses the guide for everyone on that server instance.
   - Fix: call `messages.create` with the format and `safeParse` the text yourself, or catch the parse error inside `claude.ts` and return refused. Map a refused or unreadable input screen to `safety`, which is the screen's own "if safety fits at all" rule. Add a `claude.ts` test with a mocked client.
3. **Focus is lost in the sheet.** At `guide-sheet.tsx:163` and `:203`, clicking "Got it" or "Start a new chat" removes the focused button. Fix: focus the textarea in an effect when `introSeen` or `full` changes.
4. **Helpline links are under 44 px** (`guide-sheet.tsx:278-286`). These are safety-critical controls for children. Fix: add `min-h-11`.
5. **The e2e spec can race.**
   - The switch updates optimistically, so `aria-checked` passes before the action commits. `reload()` and the child's navigation can then race it (`guide.spec.ts:90-92, 172-179`).
   - `toHaveCount(0)` at `:85` and `:179` can pass before `HomeGuide`'s Suspense boundary has streamed in.
   - Fix: `await expect(toggle).toBeEnabled()` after each click. Before the negative checks, `waitForLoadState("networkidle")` or wait for something visible inside the same boundary.
6. **Parent chat times have no timezone** (`parent-views.tsx:9-14`), so they show in server time (UTC on Vercel). Fix: pass the family timezone; `loadChild` already returns the family context.

### Nit

- `pipeline.ts:86-89`: a safety reply waits for the main Opus call to settle, up to about 40 s. Return as soon as the screen says it is not on topic, and abort the main call.
- `guide-sheet.tsx:98-122`: if the child closes and reopens the sheet while a send is in flight, the old result lands in the new chat. Guard with a generation ref.
- A JA409 from the database maps to "That already exists." for the child. Map it to `guideCopy.chatFull` in the repository. Validation failures show the generic "Please fix the highlighted fields."; show `fieldErrors.text` instead.
- `claude.ts:26-31`: the local `Usage` type and its casts are redundant (`Anthropic.Usage` has those fields). The comment at `:91-92` says earlier turns are cached, but only the system block has a cache breakpoint.
- Unused code:
  - `BuiltContext.label` duplicates `labelFor`.
  - `GUIDE_PROMPT_VERSION` is never stored or logged.
  - `routes.safety` is never linked; link it from the switch's consent text.
- The `/safety` page hardcodes the helpline numbers; render them from `helplinesFor`.
- `countFlaggedChats` and `getGuideTranscript` load up to 50 chats and all their messages just to count or find one. Chats beyond 50 can't be opened. Add a head-count query and a single-conversation read.
- `GuideSwitch` is 32 px tall.
- `CheckQuestion`: `key={option}` can repeat, and disabling the options after a pick drops focus.
- The intro card reappears each time `StepGuide` remounts on a new step, until the child sends a message.
- The breaker tests live in `cost.test.ts`; move them to `breaker.test.ts`.
- `guide.eval.ts:129`: a missing (`null`) judge verdict counts as "no break". Make it fail the gate.

**Verdict:** request changes. Fix the RLS leak (1) and the refusal/parse handling (2) before merging.

Review of `supabase/migrations/20261030120000_guide.sql` (G below) and `supabase/tests/database/guide.test.sql`. I checked the branch for hidden code first and found none. One blocking problem; it can be fixed in the same file because nothing has been applied to production yet.

**Blocking**

1. G:94-96: a parent can read the child's message when it got a safety response. ADR 0010:17 and `src/app/(marketing)/safety/page.tsx:52` promise they can't. Only the app hides it (`hideSafety`, service.ts:252), so a parent can query `guide_messages` over the REST API with their own session.
```sql
parent_hidden boolean not null default false,   -- table column
-- guide_record_turn, child row:
insert into public.guide_messages (conversation_id, child_id, role, text, parent_hidden)
values (p_conversation_id, p_child_id, 'child', p_child_text, coalesce(p_route = 'safety', false));
create policy "Guide messages are viewable by the child" on public.guide_messages
  for select to authenticated using (public.is_self(child_id));
create policy "Guide messages are viewable by parents except safety disclosures" on public.guide_messages
  for select to authenticated using (public.is_parent_of(child_id) and not parent_hidden);
```
The app then shows the placeholder before a `safety` reply that has no visible child row, in the transcript and the preview.

**Should fix**

2. G:155, G:197, G:123: start, record_turn and set_guide_access read the membership row without locking it. A call that races `remove_child` can create a chat or switch row after its deletes, which ADR 0007 rules out. record_turn doesn't check membership at all. Lock membership first in all three, before the chat row, because `remove_child` locks the membership row and then deletes chats; the other order deadlocks:
```sql
select fm.family_id into v_family_id from public.family_members fm
 where fm.profile_id = p_child_id and fm.role = 'child' and fm.status = 'approved' for share;
if v_family_id is null then raise exception using errcode = 'JA403', message = 'Not allowed'; end if;
```
3. G:127-134: the audit row is written even when the upsert's WHERE skipped the update, so a double click records consent twice. `get diagnostics v_rows = row_count; if v_rows > 0 then insert into public.audit_log …; end if;`
4. G:50 vs `provider.ts:10`: the reply is an unbounded `z.string()` with `max_tokens` 2048. A reply over 2000 characters raises 23514 after three paid calls, the turn is lost, and its tokens never reach `guide_usage_since`, so the budget undercounts. Cap it in the schema (an over-long reply becomes fixed copy) and check early: `if char_length(p_reply_text) > 2000 or char_length(p_child_text) > 2000 then raise … '22023'`. The 12-turn race has the same budget hole: two asks at turns=11 both pay and the second gets JA409. The database cap itself holds (`for update` plus the check constraint).
5. G:42: the index can't serve `order by flagged desc, last_message_at desc`. `countFlaggedChats` sorts all of a child's chats on every parent card. `create index guide_conversations_child_list_idx on public.guide_conversations (child_id, flagged desc, last_message_at desc);` replaces it. Don't make it partial, because it also covers the child_id foreign key.
6. G:208-231: both messages get `now()`, the transaction start time. A turn that waited on the lock can get an earlier time than the turn that committed before it, so the transcript is out of order. After the lock, set `v_at := clock_timestamp()` and use it for both `created_at` values and `last_message_at`. The role tie-break (child < fixed_copy < guide) then holds.

**Nits**

- G:198: a null `p_child_id` passes `<>`. Use `is distinct from`.
- G:217-222: `::integer` fails on `800.0`, huge or negative values, and an array silently gives zeros. Raise 22023 unless `jsonb_typeof(v_usage) = 'object'`, then use `least(greatest(coalesce((v_usage->>'main_input')::numeric,0),0),2147483647)::int`.
- G:264: a null `p_since` returns no rows, so the budget breaker fails open. Raise on null.
- G:51: add `check ((role = 'child') = (route is null))`.
- G:47: add a composite foreign key like `step_results` has, so a message's `child_id` always matches its chat: `unique (id, child_id)` on conversations, and `foreign key (conversation_id, child_id) references public.guide_conversations (id, child_id) on delete cascade`.
- G:32: `check (context_key ~ '^[a-z0-9:-]+$')` enforces "never free text".
- G:319: set `finished_at` like 130003 does. Add a partial index `(created_at) where text_blanked_at is null` for the retention job.
- repository.ts:159: `count: 'exact'` counts every message on each child page load. Use `.limit(1)`.
- ADR 0007: add an amendment line for ADR 0010.

**Checked and fine**

- RLS on all three tables, with SELECT-only grants.
- Function grants: anon revoked everywhere; the server functions are service_role only; the retention job has no grants.
- `search_path = ''` on every function.
- The cron block matches 130003.
- `remove_child` differs from the learning version only by the two guide deletes, and `create or replace` keeps its grant from 20261029100000:216-225.
- `is_parent_of` requires an approved child.
- Ratings are limited to a child's own `guide` replies.
- Types are regenerated.

**Missing from the pgTAP file**

- Checks that RLS is on, plus `policies_are`, for the three tables.
- EXECUTE privileges for anon and authenticated on every function (only two are checked now).
- After a switch-off, starting a chat is refused.
- A toggle that changes nothing is not audited.
- Turn 11 to 12 succeeds (the test only sets 12 by hand).
- Within a turn, the child's message comes before the reply.
- `guide_usage_since` leaves out child rows and earlier days.
- A 29-day-old message is not blanked.
- A `cron.job` row exists when pg_cron is installed.
- A parent can't rate, and fixed copy can't be rated.
- A removed child's parent reads nothing.
- Running `remove_child` twice works.
- After fix 1: the parent can't see the safety child row, and the child still can.

timestamp: 2026-10-30T07:06:00.430Z
time: 2026-10-30 07:06
chars: 4382

Mastra vs AI SDK Anthropic provider: findings. Pages read: ai-sdk.dev/providers/ai-sdk-providers/anthropic; mastra.ai/docs/agents/overview, /agents/structured-output, /agents/processors, /agents/guardrails, /frameworks/web-frameworks/next-js; github.com/mastra-ai/mastra/releases. Items marked [summary] came from a model-extracted summary of the truncated part of the page, not verbatim text.

A. AI SDK Anthropic provider (@ai-sdk/anthropic)
1. Native structured output: yes. The page says the default `"auto"` mode uses native structured outputs via `output_config.format` for claude-sonnet-5-5, claude-opus-5-5 and claude-fable-5-1, and that those models reject forced tool use. Control option: `structuredOutputMode`, values `"outputFormat" | "jsonTool" | "auto"` (auto is the default). The option's own description says `output_format`, while the models section says `output_config.format`. Other models fall back to `"jsonTool"`.
2. Effort: yes. `effort: "low" | "medium" | "high" | "xhigh" | "max"` under `providerOptions.anthropic`.
3. Prompt caching [summary]: `providerOptions: { anthropic: { cacheControl: { type: 'ephemeral' } } }` on a message or part, a system message, or a tool. `ttl: '1h'` for a 1-hour cache.
4. Mid-conversation system message [summary]: supported and not hoisted, but needs `allowSystemInMessages: true`.
5. Refusal: a classifier block returns API `stop_reason: "refusal"`, which the SDK surfaces as finish reason `content-filter`, with details on `providerMetadata.anthropic.stopDetails`. The page says to branch on the finish reason, not on `stop_details`.
6. Cache tokens [summary]: `result.usage.inputTokenDetails.cacheReadTokens` and `.cacheWriteTokens`.

B. Mastra structured output
Option: `structuredOutput: { schema, ... }` on `agent.generate()` or `stream()`. The result is `response.object`. Other options: `model`, `useAgent`, `jsonPromptInjection` (`false | 'inline' | true | 'system' | 'auto'`), `instructions`, `errorStrategy` (`'strict' | 'warn' | 'fallback'`), `fallbackValue`. By default the schema goes to the provider as the `response_format` API parameter, so it is native. The page does not say it maps to Anthropic's `output_config.format`. If `model` is set, a second agent makes a second LLM call to structure the text, which adds latency and cost.

C. Processors and guardrails
Built-ins named: UnicodeNormalizer, PromptInjectionDetector, LanguageDetector, BatchPartsProcessor, SystemPromptScrubber, ModerationProcessor, PIIDetector, TokenCostControl. Also TokenLimiter, ToolCallFilter, ToolSearchProcessor, ProviderHistoryCompat, PrefillErrorHandler, StreamErrorRetryProcessor, UnsupportedFileHandler, ResponseCache.
LLM-based: PromptInjectionDetector, LanguageDetector, SystemPromptScrubber, ModerationProcessor and PIIDetector each take a `model` and make an extra model call. TokenCostControl reads cost data from observability storage and makes no model call. The other processors' call behaviour is not stated (TokenLimiter and UnicodeNormalizer look rule-based, but the page does not say so).
Custom abort: yes. `processInput()` receives an `abort()` function, and `abort(reason, options)` takes a plain string reason. Stream clients get a `tripwire` chunk, and `generate()` returns `result.tripwire` with `reason` and `processorId`.

D. Mastra in Next.js
Setup: `npx mastra@latest init` creates `src/mastra/index.ts`, which holds the "Mastra config, including memory". Route handler: `handleChatStream({ mastra, agentId, version: 'v7', params })` from `@mastra/ai-sdk`, plus a `createNextRouteHandler({ mastra })` catch-all from `@mastra/next`. Node.js `v22.13.0` or later is required.
`serverExternalPackages`: not mentioned anywhere on the page.
Storage: the page shows a `file:` storage URL (`file:/absolute/path/to/your/project/mastra.db`) in a Studio note. It does not say a storage backend is required for memory, and does not name LibSQL or Postgres as required.
Serverless/Vercel: the page only links to "Deploy your agent to Vercel" (mastra.ai/integrations/deploy/vercel), which I did not fetch. It does not discuss serverless limits or file-based storage on Vercel.

E. Latest @mastra/core: 1.80.0, released October 28, 2026 (GitHub releases page, marked Latest).

Caveat: the Anthropic structured-output section and the processor and guardrail lists were partly read from summaries of truncated text.

## Security audit: `feat/jarso-agent` (working tree compared with `main`)

**Nothing blocking.** The main risks you asked about are closed. I found no way for the model or the browser to choose whose data gets read, no way for planned gifts or another child's data to reach a child, and no secrets in the diff. Below are 4 should-fix items and 4 nits.

I worked read-only (git, grep and file reads). I ran nothing from the project and opened no `.env*` files.

**Obfuscation scan first (your standing rule): clean.** No `eval(`, `new Function(`, `child_process`, hex/XOR arrays, RPC URLs, `.bat` files or `.gitignore` changes. Every line over 500 characters is Markdown prose in the docs. The `next.config.ts` change is only the `"*.md": { type: "raw" }` rule. I skipped the `gh api …/activity` force-push check because it needs the network and your brief limited me to git, grep and file reads.

### What I checked and found correct
- **Whose data is read.** `childId` comes only from `assertRole("child")` in `service.ts:164` and goes to `createTurnTools` at `service.ts:241-244`. Tool inputs carry no ids: `period` is an enum and `price`/`save_each` are strings of at most 20 characters, checked again with Zod. Every runner calls a service that runs `assertSelfOrParentOf` and then uses the cookie (RLS) client. `conversationId` is checked against the session child (`service.ts:185-188`). `guide_find_turn` filters on `p_child_id`. `facts` is a new `Map` for every turn, so nothing carries over between requests.
- **Planned gifts.** `parent_gifts` has a parent-only SELECT policy. `listDueForChild` sums only `allowance_occurrences`. A gift reaches the ledger only at handover, and from then on the child can see it anyway.
- **Look-ups wait for the screen.** `pipeline.ts:170-180,202` holds every tool until the input screen says on-topic. The abort runs in the same tick as `release(false)`. The 6-call cap holds when calls run in parallel, because the check and the push happen before any await.
- **Fake refs.** `cleanLabel` strips `{}`. Every fact value is formatted money, a date or a count, so nothing user-typed is filled in. Unknown or malformed refs are blocked. The output screen sees the filled text.
- **SQL.** All three functions use `security definer` with `set search_path = ''`. `create or replace` keeps the existing grants on `set_guide_access` (authenticated) and `guide_start_conversation` (service_role). The new 12-argument `guide_record_turn` revokes `public`/`anon`/`authenticated` and grants only `service_role`. The consent logic matches in `set_guide_access`, `guide_start_conversation` (`>= 2`) and `findGuideAccess`, and nothing else reads `guide_access`.
- **Route.** `withApi` runs the same-origin check and returns safe error mapping. The stream sends only `AppError` safe messages and logs class names only. `cache-control: no-store` is set.
- **Logs and secrets.** Logs carry look-up names only, never message text or tool results. The diff and new files contain no credential-shaped strings; the only emails are a GitHub noreply address and the test string `[email redacted]`. No new credential type, so the scanners need no change.
- **Rendering.** No `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`; replies render as React text.

### Should fix
1. **The database test for "only the server records turns" now fails, and the new function's grants are untested** (Medium). `supabase/tests/database/guide.test.sql:32-34` still checks the 11-argument signature, which `20261030140000_jarso_agent.sql:139` drops. pgTAP will report that function as missing, so no test covers the grants on the 12-argument function. There are also no DB tests for:
   - a v1 row → `guide_start_conversation` refuses with JA403;
   - `set_guide_access(true)` on an enabled v1 row → upgrades to v2 and writes an audit row;
   - `p_tool_calls` rejections (not an array, more than 6, name too long, set on a child row).

   **Fix:** change the assertion to the 12-argument signature and add those cases.

2. **Model spend is under-counted, so the daily and monthly caps can be overrun** (Low). `claude.ts:159-181` keeps token counts in a local `counted`, and any throw (deadline, network, the abort at `pipeline.ts:204`) discards them. The pipeline then records only the screens' tokens (`pipeline.ts:208-212`). Example: a Sonnet round 0 finishes with a `tool_use`, then the screen routes the message off-topic, or round 2 hits the 45 s deadline. Those rounds are paid but never reach `guide_usage_since`. Rate limits keep the gap small, and the Anthropic Console spend limit is the backstop. **Fix:** pass a usage accumulator into `reply()`, or attach partial usage to the thrown error, and add it on every pipeline path.

3. **`tool_calls` escapes the 30-day retention published on /safety** (Low). `blank_old_guide_text` (`20261030120000_guide.sql:369`) blanks only `text`. `/safety` (`page.tsx:75`) says what remains after 30 days is timing, handling and word counts. But `tool_calls.input` keeps the `plan_saving` `price`/`save_each` strings indefinitely, and the model takes those from the child's message (`tool-specs.ts:96-99`). **Fix:** have the job strip `input` too (keep `[{name}]`), or update the published text.

4. **An example in Jarso's voice file teaches the model to compare money itself and makes up the result** (Low; FR07 says figures come from the app). `instructions.md:53-54` reads: "can i buy a game for 15 pounds → You have {{jars.spend}} in Spend, and the game costs more than that." The conclusion is fixed whatever the real balance is, and it contradicts `prompts.ts:55`. A child with £20 in Spend could be told a £15 game costs more. The output screen's `invented_figure` check would not catch it, because no figure is stated. **Fix:** state the figure and let the child compare ("Is that enough for it?"), or do the comparison in code (for example a Spend comparison in `plan_saving`). Then bump `GUIDE_PROMPT_VERSION` and re-run the evals.

### Nits
5. **Typed labels skip the input screen.** Up to 10 labels of 40 characters each go to the model without passing the input screen: purchase labels the child typed and parents' gift notes (`tools.ts:62-65,179`). Braces are stripped and contact details scrubbed, and the system prompt and output screen still apply. Two limits remain:
   - A child can use their own purchase labels to get round the jailbreak route.
   - A worrying message written in a label gets a model reply rather than the fixed safety reply with helplines.

   It only affects the child's own session; the eval fixture plants one such label. **Optional fix:** run labels through `looksUnsafe` and drop any that trip it. Gift notes can contain names; send them only if the question needs them.
6. **`p_tool_calls` validation lets an element with no `name` through.** At `20261030140000_jarso_agent.sql:181`, `jsonb_typeof(NULL) <> 'string'` evaluates to NULL, so the check passes. Only the server writes this, so it isn't exploitable. **Fix:** use `coalesce(jsonb_typeof(call->'name'),'') <> 'string'` and optionally limit names to the six tools.
7. **Two public claims say more than the code guarantees.**
   - ADR 0011:14 says a message routed elsewhere "sends nothing about the child's money to the model". Earlier on-topic replies, which contain filled-in figures, still go to the main model before the screen decides, and the last 4 turns go to the input screen. That data was already sent with consent.
   - `/safety` says a child "never" sees a made-up balance. A figure the model writes as plain text is caught only by the Haiku output screen, which can miss. Soften both wordings.
8. **The session log names your other GitHub account.** `.claude-logs/2026-10-28_16-17-30_38042c5e-….md:3865,3882` adds more lines naming [private] and [private], and quotes your private global rules. This is not a credential, the same thing already appears earlier in the log, and the question of redacting it is still open. Decide before committing whether to redact it or add those terms to `REDACTIONS` in `.claude/hooks/extract-log.py`.

### Relevant files

- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/migrations/20261030140000_jarso_agent.sql

## Review: Jarso agent working tree on `feat/jarso-agent`, compared with `main`

**Not ready to merge: 2 blocking issues.** Neither is a layering or barrel problem. The first makes Jarso show a child the wrong figure; the second fails CI.

I ran only git, grep and file reads; no project code was executed. Before reviewing I scanned the changed and untracked files for hidden or obfuscated code and found nothing. The three lines over 500 characters are the eval judge prompt and generated `database.types.ts`; no `eval`/`Function`/`child_process`/RPC URLs, no `.gitignore` change.

### Blocking

1. **Figure refs collide when a tool runs twice in one turn, so the app fills in the wrong figure.**
   - Where: `src/features/guide/server/facts.ts:22` (`facts.set(key, shown)` overwrites with no check) and the fixed keys in `src/features/guide/server/tools.ts` (172, 183, 188–190, 255–270).
   - Those keys don't change with the arguments: `history.spent`, `history.money_in`, `history.purchases`, `history.N.date/amount`, `plan.price`, `plan.still_needed`, `plan.allowances`.
   - Example: "Did I spend more this week or last week?" calls `get_money_history` twice (allowed: up to 6 look-ups). Both results carry `{{history.spent}}` with different values. The model can only write that one ref, and `fillFacts` puts the last-written value in both places.
   - The output screen only sees the surviving value through `describeFacts`, so it passes. The child reads the wrong amount for one of the weeks, which is the failure ADR 0011 and the `/safety` page ("a child never sees a made-up balance") promise can't happen.
   - Fix:
     - Put the arguments in the keys, e.g. `history.${period}.spent`, `history.${period}.${n}.date`, and a per-call counter for `plan_saving` (`plan.${k}.price`).
     - Make `addFact` refuse to rebind an existing key to a different value, so `withFacts` holds the reply back.
     - Add a tools test and a comparison eval case.

2. **A pgTAP test still checks the dropped 11-argument signature, so CI fails.**
   - Where: `supabase/tests/database/guide.test.sql:32-34` runs `function_privs_are` on `guide_record_turn` with the 11-argument list. The migration drops that signature (`supabase/migrations/20261030140000_jarso_agent.sql:139`).
   - pgTAP fails `function_privs_are` when the function doesn't exist, and CI runs `supabase test db --local` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:95`).
   - Fix: add `'jsonb'` to the argument array. Also assert `service_role` EXECUTE on the new signature.

### Should fix

3. **A client disconnect mid-turn trips the circuit breaker.**
   - Where: `src/features/guide/server/stream.ts:16-17`. `send` calls `controller.enqueue` unguarded, and `onStatus` is called outside the `try` in `tools.ts:309`.
   - If the response body is cancelled (child closes the tab, connection drops), `enqueue` throws `TypeError`. That escapes `tools.run`, breaking the "`run` never throws" contract in `provider.ts:19`, so `Promise.all` in `claude.ts:195` rejects.
   - The pipeline then returns `failed: true`, `runGuideTurn` calls `guideBreaker.recordFailure()`, and a misleading "Guide turn failed" error is logged. Five of these in a minute pause Jarso for everyone.
   - Fix: wrap `send` in a try/catch (or track `cancel()`) so it is a no-op once the stream is closed. Move `onStatus` inside the runner's `try` as well.

4. **The last round discards paid work and shows the wrong message.**
   - Where: `src/features/guide/server/claude.ts:189-193`. The 4th call still offers tools; if the model asks for one, the turn becomes `refused`.
   - The child then sees "Jarso is taking a break", after up to 4 paid Sonnet calls.
   - Fix: when `round === MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS - 1`, add a text block after the tool results ("No more look-ups this turn; answer with what you have"). Alternatively send `tool_choice: { type: "none" }` on the final call; the tools and system prefix stay cached.

5. **No tests for the two new stream pieces.** `src/features/guide/send-turn.ts` and `src/features/guide/server/stream.ts` have none; `ask-jarso.test.tsx` mocks `sendGuideTurn` away.
   - Add tests for the reader: a JSON line split across chunks, multibyte text split across chunks (decoder flush), the `error` event, a non-2xx JSON envelope with `fieldErrors`, and a stream that ends early.
   - Add tests for the stream: status lines in order before the reply, AppError becoming an `error` event, an unknown error becoming `INTERNAL` with a log, and the `stored` path.

6. **pgTAP doesn't cover the new database rules.** Add tests for:
   - `guide_start_conversation` refusing a child switched on under consent version 1.
   - `set_guide_access` upgrading version 1 to 2 and auditing it once.
   - `guide_record_turn` rejecting `tool_calls` that has more than 6 items, contains a non-object, or is set on a child row.
   - Remember to bump `plan(67)`.

7. **`docs/architecture.md` contradicts the code.**
   - Lines 155–156: the module graph still shows `guide (later)` with edges to learning and ledger only. `tools.ts` now also imports `allowance/server` and `rules/server`, and the service imports `family/server`.
   - Line 53 still quotes "The guide is taking a break"; the copy is now "Jarso is taking a break".

8. **Duplicated week-start logic.** `src/features/guide/server/tools.ts:72-73` works out Monday by hand, re-implementing `startOfWeek` from `@/lib` (`src/lib/dates.ts:39`). `getLearningOverview` already uses `startOfWeek` for "days this week". Use it so the two weeks can't drift apart.

### Nit

- `src/features/guide/copy.ts:19`: `homeHint` is never used.
- `vitest.config.mts:8-14` and `vitest.eval.config.mts:7-13`: the `markdownAsText` plugin is copied verbatim. Move it into one shared module.
- `tools.ts:300`: rebuilds the list of tool names; use `toolNames` from `tool-specs.ts`.
- `tools.ts:44-59`: `FREQUENCY_TEXT` and `showDate` repeat `describeCadence(frequency, anchorOn)` and `formatDay` from `@/features/allowance`. Reusing them would also make Jarso say "Every Sunday", matching the child's allowance card.
- `context.ts`: `buildContext(..., today?)` takes `today` as optional only so old tests compile. Make it required.
- `guide-sheet.tsx:49-50`: the comment line is 126 characters and still mentions the "AI guide" label (now "Jarso AI").
- `claude.ts:161-176`: the ADR says "adaptive thinking", but no `thinking` field is sent (the test asserts it is undefined). The SDK says what happens when it is omitted depends on the model. Add a one-line comment, or set `{ type: "adaptive" }` explicitly.
- `docs/adr/0011-jarso-agent.md:14` says a message routed off-topic "sends nothing about the child's money". From turn 2 on, earlier on-topic replies with real figures filled in go to the main call before the screen's verdict (`pipeline.ts:181`, `service.ts:84-92`). Reword the ADR.
- `evals/guide/fixture-tools.ts`: skips the real toolbox's 6-call cap, its Zod argument check and `parseMoney`. For example, it accepts `"12.505"`, which production refuses, so the gate doesn't exercise them.
- `send-turn.ts:6-11`: `GuideTurnRequest` is hand-written; derive it from `askGuideSchema`'s input type so the two can't drift.

### Checked and fine

- **Barrels:** the server barrel starts with `server-only`. Nothing server-only is reachable from `ask-jarso.tsx`, `guide-reply.tsx` or `send-turn.ts`. Cross-module imports go through barrels.
- **Auth:** the route goes through `prepareGuideTurn`, whose `assertRole("child")` calls `assertUser`. Every service a tool calls checks `assertSelfOrParentOf`, and the child id comes only from the session.
- **Layering:** only `repository.ts` touches tables. The migration repeats the grants, keeps a default on the new argument so the old call still works, and the generated types are updated.
- **Tool loop:** the assistant content (thinking included) goes back unchanged, all tool results go in one user message right after it, and the system context sits after the child's message. The 6-call cap holds under `Promise.all`.
- **Stream reader:** partial lines are buffered and the decoder is flushed when the stream ends.
- **React state:** updates after the await are wrapped in a new `startTransition`; the generation guard drops stale replies; double submits are blocked.
- **Rendering:** Ask Jarso is inside `<Suspense>` on the child's Home.
- **Turbopack:** the `*.md` `type: "raw"` rule is valid for Next 16.2+ (16.4.3 is installed).

Relevant files:
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/guide/server/facts.ts
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/guide/server/tools.ts
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/tests/database/guide.test.sql
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/guide/server/stream.ts
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/guide/server/claude.ts
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/guide/send-turn.ts
- /Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/docs/architecture.md

# Security audit: `feat/web-push` (FR16, ADR 0012), uncommitted changes

Scope: `git status --short`, `git diff`, every untracked file, plus `docs/auth.md`, the `docs/database.md` diff and REQUIREMENTS FR16/FR07-09. Read-only: git, grep, cat, and one `python3 -I` regex check on placeholder strings I wrote myself (no repo code executed, no pnpm/node/supabase).

Pre-work obfuscation scan (standing rule): no `eval(`, `new Function`, `child_process`, `windowsHide`, `global[...]`, RPC URLs or `.bat` files anywhere in the diff or new files; lines over 500 chars occur only in markdown prose, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `scripts/check-secrets.sh` (the existing single-line regex) and generated `src/types/database.types.ts`. Nothing suspicious.

## Verdict

**Nothing is blocking.** The RLS/grant model, the server-only split, the cron auth and the secret handling are correct. Four things are worth fixing before merge (one of them a genuine abuse path, one a public-repo scanner gap the new docs actively steer people into), and a handful of nits.

## Blocking

None.

## Should fix

### S1. No cap on subscriptions per profile, no concurrency cap on sends: a single member can amplify and starve other families' pushes
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/migrations/20261031080000_push_subscriptions.sql:35-73` (`push_subscribe` has no per-profile limit; `endpoint` is unique but nothing bounds rows per `profile_id`)
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/push.ts:74-96` (`Promise.all` over every notice x every subscription with no concurrency limit)
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/actions.ts:18-24` (no rate limit on `subscribePushAction`; and `push_subscribe` is `authenticated`-callable straight through PostgREST, so Zod isn't a gate anyway)

Scenario: any approved member (a child included) registers thousands of distinct `https://` endpoints under their own account. Every notice to them then fans out into thousands of outbound HTTPS POSTs inside `after()` or the 30 s cron. Because `claim_unpushed_notifications` already stamped `pushed_at` on the whole batch (up to 50/200 notices across all families), a function that times out mid-batch silently drops other families' pushes forever (no retry by design). The outbound request also goes to whatever host they named (ciphertext body + audience-bound VAPID JWT, so no data leaks; the cost is the fan-out, not SSRF).

Fix: in `push_subscribe`, after the insert, delete the caller's rows beyond the newest N (10 is plenty for one person's devices); in `dispatchPush`, cap per-recipient subscriptions and run sends through a small concurrency limiter (or chunk `Promise.all`). Optionally rate-limit the action like the PIN flow does.

### S2. The three secret scanners miss the exact formats the new docs tell people to generate
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/.env.example:29` and `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/docs/deploy.md:30` instruct `pnpm exec web-push generate-vapid-keys`
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/.gitleaks.toml` (new `vapid-private-key` rule), `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/scripts/check-secrets.sh:6`, `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/.claude/hooks/extract-log.py` REDACTIONS (KEY=value rule around line 53-57, JSON-literal rule around line 59)

All three anchor on the literal `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` variable name (or `PRIVATE_KEY` on the same line as the value). The CLI's human output puts the key on the line *after* `Private Key:`, and its `--json` output is `{"publicKey":"…","privateKey":"…"}`; neither `privateKey` nor a next-line value is matched by any rule (the JSON rule's name list is password|passwd|secret|token|api_key|client_secret only). I confirmed with the regex text against 43-char placeholders: `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=…` is caught by hook+gitleaks+check-secrets; `Private Key:\n…`, `"privateKey":"…"` and `printf '…' | vercel env add VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY …` are caught by none. A VAPID private key has no prefix, so if that output is ever pasted into a prompt, commit or doc, the public `.claude-logs/` would carry it unredacted. The ADR (0012, "Consequences") claims the three scanners cover it; today that is only true for the `.env` line form.

Fix: add to all three scanners (a) `(?i)private[ _-]?key["']?\s*:\s*["']?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}` for the JSON/camelCase form, (b) a multi-line form `Private Key:\s*\r?\n\s*[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}` (the hook redacts whole transcripts so a DOTALL/multiline pattern works there; gitleaks supports `\n` in `regex`), and (c) a bearer-less `vercel env add VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` form or, simpler, document `… | vercel env add VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY production` with the key read from a file rather than typed. Add the matching cases to the hook's test fixtures.

### S3. Service worker `notificationclick` will navigate to an absolute off-origin URL in the payload
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/public/sw.js:27-35`

`new URL(data.url ?? "/", self.location.origin)` only *defaults* the origin; an absolute `https://evil.example/...` in `data.url` resolves as-is, and both `WindowClient.navigate()` and `clients.openWindow()` will go there. Today the payload is server-authored (`noticeHref` returns typed relative routes) and end-to-end encrypted (RFC 8291), so it is not exploitable without the VAPID private key *and* the browser's keys. For a children's app the one-line origin check is cheap defence in depth against a future `noticeHref` regression or a key leak turning pushes into phishing redirects.

Fix: `const target = new URL(...); const url = target.origin === self.location.origin ? target.href : self.location.origin + "/";`

### S4. A removed member can still receive pushes for up to 24 h
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/supabase/migrations/20261031080000_push_subscriptions.sql:131-160` (`claim_unpushed_notifications` selects by `pushed_at is null` and age only; the `family_members` join at :158 is a `left join` with no `status = 'approved'`/`family_id` filter)

The inbox's RLS requires `is_approved_member(family_id)` to *see* a notice, but the dispatcher bypasses RLS and pushes every unpushed row. A child removed by a parent (identity kept because of money history, so `on delete cascade` doesn't fire) with an unpushed `allowance_due`/`handover_reminder` from earlier that day gets it pushed by the 01:30 sweep. The trailing comment in the migration ("nothing is pushed to it because notify_* writes no notice for a removed member") covers only notices written *after* removal.

Fix: in the `claimed` CTE add `and exists (select 1 from public.family_members m where m.profile_id = u.recipient_id and m.family_id = u.family_id and m.status = 'approved')` (still stamping the others so they are not re-scanned), or filter in the final select.

## Nit

- **Toggle state can lie after a takeover.** `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/components/push-toggle.tsx:63` derives "on" from the browser's own `getSubscription()`, not from the server row. On a shared browser profile, after the child signs in and `push_subscribe` moves the endpoint (:60 in the migration), the parent's toggle still reads "This device gets a ping" while they get none. Also `turnOff` (:117-120) unsubscribes the browser, killing the endpoint for the other account too (self-heals via 404/410). Consider an owner-scoped `hasSubscription(endpoint)` check on mount, or re-subscribing silently on sign-in.
- **DB does not enforce `https://` on `endpoint`** (`migration:8`); Zod does (`src/features/notifications/schemas.ts`), but `push_subscribe` is callable directly. web-push always uses `https.request` regardless of scheme (checked `node_modules/web-push/src/web-push-lib.js:347-369`), so a non-https row only produces a failed send, never plaintext. Add `check (endpoint like 'https://%')` for parity.
- **Empty-string env values crash boot.** `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/config/env.server.ts:31-35`: `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=""` or `CRON_SECRET=""` on the host fails `min(1)`/`min(16)` and throws for the whole server, whereas `env.client.ts` normalises with `|| undefined`. Same pattern as the pre-existing optional vars, but `CRON_SECRET` with `min(16)` is a new deploy footgun; normalise empty to undefined.
- **`logger.error("Push dispatch failed", { error })`** at `push.ts:107` serialises the full `AppError` including its PostgREST `cause` (details/hint), which `src/lib/db-error.ts:27-31` deliberately avoids elsewhere. Log `{ code: error.code }` for consistency.
- `last_used_at` is set only in the `on conflict ... do update` branch (`migration:64-69`), never on the first insert.
- `useEffect(..., [])` at `push-toggle.tsx:50-73` reads `publicKey` without listing it; harmless because the env is build-time constant, but Biome may flag it.

## Verified OK (no action)

- **RLS/grants:** table has RLS on, `revoke all from anon, authenticated` then `grant select` only, single owner-scoped select policy, no write policies (`migration:20-26`). `push_subscribe`/`push_unsubscribe` granted to `authenticated` only (:93-96); `claim_unpushed_notifications` and `push_subscriptions_for` revoked from `public, anon, authenticated`, granted to `service_role` (:165-166, :182-183). All four are `security definer` with `set search_path = ''`. pgTAP (`supabase/tests/database/push.test.sql`) covers child-cannot-see-parent-row, non-member 42501, and authenticated callers getting 42501 on the service functions.
- **Takeover semantics:** hijack requires knowing the victim's endpoint URL, which is a capability URL visible only to its owner (RLS) and inside that browser; cross-family delivery is impossible because `dispatchPush` maps each claimed notice to `byRecipient.get(claimed.recipientId)` only, and `family_members.profile_id` is `unique` so the role join yields one row.
- **Payload/logs:** payload = inbox `noticeText` (nickname, formatted amount, emergency reason), a relative `noticeHref`, and the notice id as `tag`; encrypted end to end so push services cannot read it. Logs carry counts and HTTP status only (`push.ts:91,97`), and `push.test.ts:141-147` asserts no endpoint/text is logged. Lock-screen display of nickname/amounts mirrors the inbox and is a product choice, not a leak.
- **Cron route** (`/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/app/api/cron/push/route.ts`): 404 when `CRON_SECRET` unset, length check + `timingSafeEqual`, 401 otherwise, GET with no cookie auth so the CSRF guard is irrelevant, `maxDuration = 30`, tests cover all three branches.
- **Secrets to client:** `@/config` barrel re-exports only `env.client`; `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` is read solely in server-only `push.ts`; the toggle reads `NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` only (intentionally public). No `sb_secret_`, `sb_publishable_`, `sk-ant-`, JWT, `ghp_`, connection-string or PEM strings in the diff or new files; the only email addresses are `example.com` fixtures in the pgTAP test and `.env.example`.
- **Zod vs DB:** endpoint `https` URL <= 2000 / DB 1..2000; keys 1..200 both sides; user agent reduced to a family string in `describeAgent` then `left(..., 200)`.
- **VAPID unset:** toggle returns `null`, `pushAfterNotices` is a no-op, `dispatchPush` returns zeros before touching the admin client, and the cron with only `CRON_SECRET` set returns zero counts. `env.client.ts` normalises empty public key to undefined.
- **Error surface:** `push_subscribe`'s 42501 has no `db-error.ts` mapping, so the user sees the generic "Failed to save the subscription" and the log keeps code+message only.
- **Headers:** no CSP exists in `next.config.ts` (pre-existing), no third-party scripts added; `sw.js` is same-origin and caches nothing.

## Blocking

None.

## Should fix

1. `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/docs/architecture.md` — still describes the ADR 0006 design that this change replaces, so the "map" now contradicts the code and ADR 0012 in about a dozen places: line 3 ("later, web push"), 39 and 52 ("Browser push (later)"), 66–67 (database webhook → `/api/hooks/notifications` on `notification_deliveries` insert; "Service worker (later)"), 98, 116 ("deliveries, channels"), 195 and 377–384 (`notification_deliveries` entity), 386–391 (`push_subscriptions` with `jsonb keys`; the migration stores `p256dh` and `auth` as text columns plus `user_agent`/`last_used_at`), 452 (RLS table lists deliveries), 556–570 and 579 (dispatcher with backoff and `dead`, `dispatch_notifications` every 10 min), 584 (cron schedule "lives in Postgres"), 611 ("No Service Worker, web manifest"), 627, 659 ("push later (ADR 0006)"). Also `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/docs/domain.md:61` ("web push + PWA later in pilot"). Fix: one doc pass replacing the deliveries/webhook story with `pushed_at` + `claim_unpushed_notifications`, `pushAfterNotices` via `after()`, the daily Vercel cron, `public/sw.js` (no caching), and the real `push_subscriptions` columns; point to ADR 0012.

2. `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/public/sw.js:30-33` — `open.navigate(url)` is fire-and-forget. `WindowClient.navigate()` rejects for a client this worker does not control, and the query uses `includeUncontrolled: true`, so for such a tab (e.g. after a hard reload that bypassed the worker) the click focuses the tab without moving it to the record and leaves an unhandled rejection in the worker. Fix: `return open.navigate(url).then((c) => c?.focus()).catch(() => self.clients.openWindow(url));`.

3. `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/proxy.ts:39` — the matcher excludes images but not `/sw.js` or `/manifest.webmanifest`, so every service-worker update check (browsers re-fetch `sw.js` on navigation, capped at 24 h) and manifest fetch runs `updateSession()` — a Supabase auth round trip for a static file. Fix: add `sw\\.js|manifest\\.webmanifest` to the negative lookahead alongside `favicon.ico`.

## Nit

- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/push.ts:67` — the early `return result` when nobody is subscribed skips `logger.info("Push dispatched", result)` at line 97, so a sweep that claimed N notices and sent none leaves no log line (the counts only surface in the cron JSON). Move the early return after the log, or log before returning.
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/push.ts:88` — `deleteSubscriptionById(...).catch(() => undefined)` swallows the failure silently; a `logger.warn("Could not drop a gone subscription")` (no id/endpoint) keeps the public-log hygiene and still tells you when cleanup breaks.
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/service.ts:591-602` and `supabase/migrations/20261031080000_push_subscriptions.sql:11` — `user_agent` is derived, truncated and stored but read nowhere (no UI lists devices). Either drop the column and `describeAgent` until a "your devices" screen exists, or leave it and accept it as speculative. Also iPadOS 13+ Safari reports `Macintosh`, so iPads will read "Safari on Macintosh".
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/components/push-toggle.tsx:50-73` — the effect reads `publicKey` with an empty dependency array. Functionally fine (it is an inlined build constant), but `[publicKey]` is the honest list and costs nothing.
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/app/api/cron/push/route.ts:9-13` — `sameSecret` is the first cron guard living in `app/`. Fine for one route; if a second `/api/cron/*` arrives, lift it into `@/lib/api` as `assertCronSecret(request)` so the 404/401 behaviour stays identical.
- `/Users/harisshakeel/Developer/Jarso/src/features/notifications/server/service.ts:577-584` — `subscribePush` returns `{ id }` that the toggle never reads. Returning `void` avoids shipping a row id to the browser for no reason.

## Confirmed fine (checked, not speculated)

- Layering: table/function names only in `repository.ts` (`push_subscribe`, `push_unsubscribe`, `claim_unpushed_notifications`, `push_subscriptions_for`, `.from("push_subscriptions")`); `push.ts` composes repository calls; the cron route and inbox pages hold no business logic.
- Barrels: `push-toggle.tsx` imports `clientEnv` from `@/config`, whose barrel is `countries`, `env.client`, `helplines`, `site` — client-safe. Its `../server/actions` import is a `"use server"` file (the standard client→action pattern, same module, relative). `requests/server/actions.ts` and `outbox/route.ts` import `pushAfterNotices` through `@/features/notifications/server`; `requests → notifications` is already an edge in the dependency graph. Both barrels stay unmixed; `push.ts`, `repository.ts`, `service.ts` start with `import "server-only"`.
- Cache Components: `PushToggle` renders inside `InboxContent`, which is inside the existing `<Suspense>` on both inbox pages; it is a client component and reads no request data.
- Auth: `subscribePush`/`unsubscribePush` call `assertUser()`; `push_subscribe` additionally requires an approved `family_members` row (`42501`); `claim_unpushed_notifications`/`push_subscriptions_for` are `service_role` only and pgTAP asserts the 42501 for a signed-in user. Cron route: 404 without `CRON_SECRET`, timing-safe bearer compare, `withApi` skips the CSRF origin check on GET so Vercel's cron request (no `Origin`) passes. Admin client is used only in `dispatchPush` with no caller-controlled filter.
- Notice writers vs `pushAfterNotices()` coverage: `create_withdrawal`, `create_deposit`, `create_emergency`, `decide_withdrawal`, `decide_deposit` (via `rejectDepositAction`) and `record_purchase` → `evaluate_spend_limits` (via the outbox route, the only `recordPurchase` caller) are all followed by `pushAfterNotices()`. `complete_request`, `cancel_request`, `undo_purchase` write no notice (checked the migrations). `allowance_due`/`handover_reminder` come from pg_cron and are left to the daily sweep, as the ADR says.
- `claim_unpushed_notifications` left-joins `family_members` on `profile_id`, which is `unique` (`20261028181734_family_core.sql:71`), so no duplicate rows / double sends.
- `after()` from `next/server` is documented for Server Functions and Route Handlers in Next 16 (`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/04-functions/after.md`); using it after a committed service call, before `refresh()`, is correct.
- Validation/errors: both new actions go through `createAction` + Zod (`pushSubscriptionSchema` enforces https and key length ≤ 200, matching the table checks); repository wraps every Supabase `error` with `toAppError`; no `console`; logs carry only status/counts and the test at `push.test.ts:141-147` asserts no endpoint or notice text is logged.
- Secrets: `.gitleaks.toml`, `scripts/check-secrets.sh` and the existing generic `*PRIVATE_KEY=` pattern in `.claude/hooks/extract-log.py:54` all catch `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=…`; `.env.example` uses placeholders only.
- Tests assert behaviour: toggle tests are role/aria/text based (hidden without key, unsupported, blocked, subscribe → server → rollback on refusal, unsubscribe order); route test covers 404/401/200 and the dispatch limit; pgTAP covers ownership, takeover of an endpoint by another account, 42501s, claim-once, the one-day cutoff and `skip locked` semantics via "second claim finds nothing". `push.test.ts` pins `setVapidDetails` args and `TTL`/`urgency` — implementation-ish but it is the contract with the push service, acceptable.
- Copy: "Pings on this device", "Get a ping when something new lands in your inbox, even when Jarso is closed.", "That didn't work. Try again in a moment." — plain words, second person, no exclamation marks, no shame words; push text reuses `noticeText`, so it is identical to the inbox row.
- Sizing: one column, three functions, no delivery table or retry worker, daily cron on Hobby, `Promise.all` over a few dozen sends — right-sized for ~60 users; nothing over-built. Manifest icons exist (`public/brand/icons/jarso-mark-{192,512}.png`), `/icon.png` is served from `src/app/icon.png`, `/dashboard` exists and is a protected prefix, `Switch` is exported from `@/components/ui`, tokens `ink-2`/`hairline`/`card` exist in `globals.css`, `#F7F5F0` is the design canvas colour.

**Verdict:** Nothing blocking. Merge after updating `docs/architecture.md` (and the one `docs/domain.md` line) to match ADR 0012; the `sw.js` click handler and proxy matcher fixes are small and worth folding in.
